CONEWANGO CREEK AT WATERBORO NY, USGS site 03013000 (USGS-03013000), stream in Chautauqua County, New York, draining 290 square miles. Daily means from 1938-09-23 to 1993-09-29, a page a water year (October to September), linked below. Discharge in cubic feet a second, gage height in feet above the gauge's datum. Its annual peaks are its floods; its field measurements are the visits that check the gauge. Revisions are USGS's own notes of the site's published history, as written.
